docs/sota-installed-manifest.md:248:### `@latest` resolution at execute time (per Wave 50 fire 8 Pattern A on Agent I F-4 — operationalizes CR-9 version-pin mandate)
docs/sota-installed-manifest.md:250:Every install command in this manifest tagged `@latest` (e.g., `npm install -g @doobidoo/mcp-memory-service@latest`, `docker pull qdrant/qdrant:latest`, `npm install -g markdownlint-cli2@latest`) carries **implicit `@latest-acknowledged-D6-risk:2026-05-06` status** — the manifest planning intent is "pull newest at install execution per CR-6, with explicit D6 today-release-auto-upgrade risk acknowledgment per CR-9". At install execution, the operator MUST:
docs/sota-installed-manifest.md:253:2. Record both the `@latest` planning intent AND the resolved `@<resolved-version>` in `docs/install-provenance.md` under the install row (per CR-6 D6 risk-acknowledged contract)
docs/sota-installed-manifest.md:254:3. Pin the manifest install command to the resolved version post-install (manifest row updated from `@latest` to `@<resolved-version>` as part of `STAGED → INSTALLED` promotion)
docs/sota-installed-manifest.md:255:4. Re-evaluation trigger: per `docs/install-from-github-discipline.md §"When to re-install"` — the resolved-version pin holds until either a re-install is needed (per upstream changelog or sibling D6 evidence) OR the row's manifest age exceeds N-day re-evaluation cadence
docs/sota-installed-manifest.md:257:**Rationale**: the manifest's `@latest` install commands ARE the SOTA-discipline contract per CR-6 fresh-from-github (do not pre-pin to stale versions in the planning document). The resolution-at-execute-time + provenance-recording gate satisfies CR-9 version-pin mandate without violating CR-6 fresh-from-github. The promotion-gate state machine above enforces this — a row cannot advance `STAGED → INSTALLED` until step 3 (resolved-version pin) lands in the manifest row.
docs/sota-installed-manifest.md:259:**Scope**: applies to ALL install commands carrying raw `@latest` AND `@HEAD`-equivalent pulls (`git clone --depth 1 ... origin/main` without SHA pin). Does NOT apply to `@<version-tag>` pinned install commands (those satisfy CR-9 directly).

Other subagent rows carry explicit `PENDING-AT-EXECUTION` gating predicate (the manifest row only advances `PLANNED → STAGED` after the SHA column shows a pinned commit hash — non-conforming `<TBD-AT-COPY>` placeholders per Agent I F-2 are no longer admissible).
238:## Promotion gates (when does a row advance status)
240:- `PLANNED` → `STAGED`: install command run, output captured to `install-provenance.md`
241:- `STAGED` → `INSTALLED-AMBER`: smoke probe completed but Probe DAG flagged P2/P3
242:- `STAGED` → `INSTALLED`: smoke probe + axis-1+2+3 PASS + Probe DAG clean
243:- `STAGED` → `REJECTED-POST-PROBE`: axis-fail or Probe DAG REJECT
244:- `STAGED` → `DEFERRED-PENDING-FIX`: upstream issue blocks install; cite-only retained as substitute
245:- `INSTALLED-AMBER` → `INSTALLED`: deferred fix landed
248:### `@latest` resolution at execute time (per Wave 50 fire 8 Pattern A on Agent I F-4 — operationalizes CR-9 version-pin mandate)
250:Every install command in this manifest tagged `@latest` (e.g., `npm install -g @doobidoo/mcp-memory-service@latest`, `docker pull qdrant/qdrant:latest`, `npm install -g markdownlint-cli2@latest`) carries **implicit `@latest-acknowledged-D6-risk:2026-05-06` status** — the manifest planning intent is "pull newest at install execution per CR-6, with explicit D6 today-release-auto-upgrade risk acknowledgment per CR-9". At install execution, the operator MUST:
252:1. Resolve the actual version (e.g., `npm view @doobidoo/mcp-memory-service version` returns `1.4.7` → install command becomes `npm install -g @doobidoo/mcp-memory-service@1.4.7`)
253:2. Record both the `@latest` planning intent AND the resolved `@<resolved-version>` in `docs/install-provenance.md` under the install row (per CR-6 D6 risk-acknowledged contract)
254:3. Pin the manifest install command to the resolved version post-install (manifest row updated from `@latest` to `@<resolved-version>` as part of `STAGED → INSTALLED` promotion)
255:4. Re-evaluation trigger: per `docs/install-from-github-discipline.md §"When to re-install"` — the resolved-version pin holds until either a re-install is needed (per upstream changelog or sibling D6 evidence) OR the row's manifest age exceeds N-day re-evaluation cadence
257:**Rationale**: the manifest's `@latest` install commands ARE the SOTA-discipline contract per CR-6 fresh-from-github (do not pre-pin to stale versions in the planning document). The resolution-at-execute-time + provenance-recording gate satisfies CR-9 version-pin mandate without violating CR-6 fresh-from-github. The promotion-gate state machine above enforces this — a row cannot advance `STAGED → INSTALLED` until step 3 (resolved-version pin) lands in the manifest row.
259:**Scope**: applies to ALL install commands carrying raw `@latest` AND `@HEAD`-equivalent pulls (`git clone --depth 1 ... origin/main` without SHA pin). Does NOT apply to `@<version-tag>` pinned install commands (those satisfy CR-9 directly).

    4: >

    6: >
    7: > - **INSTALL-CLASS** — code/binary that RUNS in the runtime (plugin / MCP server / CLI binary / direct .claude/* import / hook script). Lives under `.claude/plugins/cache/` / `.mcp.json` / system PATH / `.claude/{rules,agents,skills,commands,hooks/scripts}/`.
    8: > - **CITE-CLASS** — documentation/guide that authorities a primitive without running. Cited at `<repo>/<file>:<line> @ HEAD <SHA>` in this manifest's "Source guide" column. Examples: CCBP `claude-settings.md`, Karpathy gist, Boris Cherny tips.

   11: 
   12: ## Status legend

   14: - `INSTALLED` — install complete + smoke-probe PASS + axis-1+2+3 verified
   15: - `INSTALLED-VIA-SYSTEM-PATH` — primitive available via system PATH (winget / chocolatey / package-manager-system-scope) at acceptable freshness per cardinal-rule-6; version anchor + smoke probe + axis verdict captured in install-provenance.md (added Wave 50 fire 4 per Agent C-redo P1-3 — fire 3 PATH-discovery introduced this disposition)
   16: - `INSTALLED-AMBER` — install complete but Probe DAG flagged P2/P3 issue; manifest carries the deferred fix
   17: - `CITE-IMPORT-AMBER` — sibling cite-import per Section 14 / 14.5 exception; subagent or hook copied with sibling commit-SHA cite trail; re-evaluate when sibling commit-SHA churns >50% post-import
   18: - `STAGED` — install command run but smoke-probe pending
   19: - `DEFERRED-PENDING-FIX` — install blocked on upstream issue (e.g., breaking change, license incompat); cite-import permitted as substitute pending fix
   20: - `REJECTED-POST-PROBE` — Probe DAG returned REJECT-FOR-FIT or axis-fail; cite-only retained for forward research
   21: - `PLANNED` — design phase; install command drafted but not yet run
   22: - `CITE-ONLY` — guide source cited as authority; no install needed (e.g., CCBP cardinal-rule cites)

  237: 
  238: ## Promotion gates (when does a row advance status)
  239: 
  240: - `PLANNED` → `STAGED`: install command run, output captured to `install-provenance.md`
  241: - `STAGED` → `INSTALLED-AMBER`: smoke probe completed but Probe DAG flagged P2/P3
  242: - `STAGED` → `INSTALLED`: smoke probe + axis-1+2+3 PASS + Probe DAG clean
  243: - `STAGED` → `REJECTED-POST-PROBE`: axis-fail or Probe DAG REJECT
  244: - `STAGED` → `DEFERRED-PENDING-FIX`: upstream issue blocks install; cite-only retained as substitute
  245: - `INSTALLED-AMBER` → `INSTALLED`: deferred fix landed
  246: - ANY → re-evaluation triggered per `docs/install-from-github-discipline.md §"When to re-install"`
  247: 
  248: ### `@latest` resolution at execute time (per Wave 50 fire 8 Pattern A on Agent I F-4 — operationalizes CR-9 version-pin mandate)
  249: 
  250: Every install command in this manifest tagged `@latest` (e.g., `npm install -g @doobidoo/mcp-memory-service@latest`, `docker pull qdrant/qdrant:latest`, `npm install -g markdownlint-cli2@latest`) carries **implicit `@latest-acknowledged-D6-risk:2026-05-06` status** — the manifest planning intent is "pull newest at install execution per CR-6, with explicit D6 today-release-auto-upgrade risk acknowledgment per CR-9". At install execution, the operator MUST:
  251: 
  252: 1. Resolve the actual version (e.g., `npm view @doobidoo/mcp-memory-service version` returns `1.4.7` → install command becomes `npm install -g @doobidoo/mcp-memory-service@1.4.7`)
  253: 2. Record both the `@latest` planning intent AND the resolved `@<resolved-version>` in `docs/install-provenance.md` under the install row (per CR-6 D6 risk-acknowledged contract)
  254: 3. Pin the manifest install command to the resolved version post-install (manifest row updated from `@latest` to `@<resolved-version>` as part of `STAGED → INSTALLED` promotion)
  255: 4. Re-evaluation trigger: per `docs/install-from-github-discipline.md §"When to re-install"` — the resolved-version pin holds until either a re-install is needed (per upstream changelog or sibling D6 evidence) OR the row's manifest age exceeds N-day re-evaluation cadence
  256: 
  257: **Rationale**: the manifest's `@latest` install commands ARE the SOTA-discipline contract per CR-6 fresh-from-github (do not pre-pin to stale versions in the planning document). The resolution-at-execute-time + provenance-recording gate satisfies CR-9 version-pin mandate without violating CR-6 fresh-from-github. The promotion-gate state machine above enforces this — a row cannot advance `STAGED → INSTALLED` until step 3 (resolved-version pin) lands in the manifest row.
  258: 
  259: **Scope**: applies to ALL install commands carrying raw `@latest` AND `@HEAD`-equivalent pulls (`git clone --depth 1 ... origin/main` without SHA pin). Does NOT apply to `@<version-tag>` pinned install commands (those satisfy CR-9 directly).

  248: ### `@latest` resolution at execute time (per Wave 50 fire 8 Pattern A on Agent I F-4 — operationalizes CR-9 version-pin mandate)
  249: 
  250: Every install command in this manifest tagged `@latest` (e.g., `npm install -g @doobidoo/mcp-memory-service@latest`, `docker pull qdrant/qdrant:latest`, `npm install -g markdownlint-cli2@latest`) carries **implicit `@latest-acknowledged-D6-risk:2026-05-06` status** — the manifest planning intent is "pull newest at install execution per CR-6, with explicit D6 today-release-auto-upgrade risk acknowledgment per CR-9". At install execution, the operator MUST:
  251: 
  252: 1. Resolve the actual version (e.g., `npm view @doobidoo/mcp-memory-service version` returns `1.4.7` → install command becomes `npm install -g @doobidoo/mcp-memory-service@1.4.7`)
  253: 2. Record both the `@latest` planning intent AND the resolved `@<resolved-version>` in `docs/install-provenance.md` under the install row (per CR-6 D6 risk-acknowledged contract)
  254: 3. Pin the manifest install command to the resolved version post-install (manifest row updated from `@latest` to `@<resolved-version>` as part of `STAGED → INSTALLED` promotion)
  255: 4. Re-evaluation trigger: per `docs/install-from-github-discipline.md §"When to re-install"` — the resolved-version pin holds until either a re-install is needed (per upstream changelog or sibling D6 evidence) OR the row's manifest age exceeds N-day re-evaluation cadence
  256: 
  257: **Rationale**: the manifest's `@latest` install commands ARE the SOTA-discipline contract per CR-6 fresh-from-github (do not pre-pin to stale versions in the planning document). The resolution-at-execute-time + provenance-recording gate satisfies CR-9 version-pin mandate without violating CR-6 fresh-from-github. The promotion-gate state machine above enforces this — a row cannot advance `STAGED → INSTALLED` until step 3 (resolved-version pin) lands in the manifest row.
  258: 
  259: **Scope**: applies to ALL install commands carrying raw `@latest` AND `@HEAD`-equivalent pulls (`git clone --depth 1 ... origin/main` without SHA pin). Does NOT apply to `@<version-tag>` pinned install commands (those satisfy CR-9 directly).

  239: ### The 4-skill stack — meta-skills active in this runtime
  240: 
  241: This runtime runs with 4 meta-skills active that govern multi-skill orchestration. They auto-fire per their `description:` triggers — no manual invocation required for the meta-layer to activate. Skill discovery is Anthropic CC native mechanism (per `https://code.claude.com/docs/en/skills`) — Claude reads `description:` frontmatter of every available SKILL.md and invokes via the `Skill` tool when description matches the current task.
  242: 
  243: | # | Meta-skill | Source plugin | Role |
  244: |---|---|---|---|
  245: | 1 | `using-superpowers` | superpowers@claude-plugins-official | "1% rule" — invoke any skill that has even a 1% chance of applying. Defines Skill Priority (Process skills first / Implementation skills second). Red Flags table catches rationalizations. |
  246: | 2 | `using-agent-skills` | agent-skills@addy-agent-skills | Discovery + invocation governance for the 21 addy engineering-phase skills. Auto-fires at session start to surface relevant skill for current task. |
  247: | 3 | `skill-comply` | everything-claude-code | Post-invocation verification that skill use was correct + complete (verifies skill checklist completion if skill body had one). |
  248: | 4 | `skill-creator` | skill-creator@claude-plugins-official | Authoring loop: draft → eval → iterate → benchmark → optimize description for triggering accuracy. Use when creating new skills OR when an existing skill should be improved. |

docs\sota-installed-manifest.md:248:### `@latest` resolution at execute time (per Wave 50 fire 8 Pattern A on Agent I F-4 — operationalizes CR-9 version-pin mandate)
docs\sota-installed-manifest.md:250:Every install command in this manifest tagged `@latest` (e.g., `npm install -g @doobidoo/mcp-memory-service@latest`, `docker pull qdrant/qdrant:latest`, `npm install -g markdownlint-cli2@latest`) carries **implicit `@latest-acknowledged-D6-risk:2026-05-06` status** — the manifest planning intent is "pull newest at install execution per CR-6, with explicit D6 today-release-auto-upgrade risk acknowledgment per CR-9". At install execution, the operator MUST:
docs\sota-installed-manifest.md:253:2. Record both the `@latest` planning intent AND the resolved `@<resolved-version>` in `docs/install-provenance.md` under the install row (per CR-6 D6 risk-acknowledged contract)
docs\sota-installed-manifest.md:254:3. Pin the manifest install command to the resolved version post-install (manifest row updated from `@latest` to `@<resolved-version>` as part of `STAGED → INSTALLED` promotion)
docs\sota-installed-manifest.md:257:**Rationale**: the manifest's `@latest` install commands ARE the SOTA-discipline contract per CR-6 fresh-from-github (do not pre-pin to stale versions in the planning document). The resolution-at-execute-time + provenance-recording gate satisfies CR-9 version-pin mandate without violating CR-6 fresh-from-github. The promotion-gate state machine above enforces this — a row cannot advance `STAGED → INSTALLED` until step 3 (resolved-version pin) lands in the manifest row.
docs\sota-installed-manifest.md:259:**Scope**: applies to ALL install commands carrying raw `@latest` AND `@HEAD`-equivalent pulls (`git clone --depth 1 ... origin/main` without SHA pin). Does NOT apply to `@<version-tag>` pinned install commands (those satisfy CR-9 directly).

[VERIFIED via TIER-1 cite] eee is operationally in `bypassPermissions`, but its manifest still has unmet CR-7 Phase 2 predicates and stale install-state rows.

**Top Findings**
1. P1: CR-7 state is inconsistent and Phase 3 is live before the manifest predicates pass.  
Live settings use `defaultMode: "bypassPermissions"` in [.claude/settings.json](Z:/claude-sota-installed/.claude/settings.json:81), while CR-7 requires Tier 0/1/2 `INSTALLED` + smoke PASS before Phase 2, and Tier 3-5 + zero `INSTALLED-AMBER` before Phase 3 in [CLAUDE.md](Z:/claude-sota-installed/CLAUDE.md:70). Current blockers include codex hooks `INSTALLED-PARTIAL` in [docs/sota-installed-manifest.md](Z:/claude-sota-installed/docs/sota-installed-manifest.md:60), `sota-researcher` `INSTALLED-AMBER` at line 213, safety hooks `INSTALLED-AMBER` at lines 196/198, and Tier 2 planned/partial rows at lines 93-94 and 136-142.

2. P1: `AGENTS.md` is a tracked hand-coded runtime artifact but is not in the allowed bootstrap set and its manifest HNF gate is still pending.  
[AGENTS.md](Z:/claude-sota-installed/AGENTS.md:14) says non-bootstrap primitives must come from upstream installs, but the bootstrap list omits `AGENTS.md`. The manifest row for AGENTS.md remains `STAGED-PENDING-HNF` in [docs/sota-installed-manifest.md](Z:/claude-sota-installed/docs/sota-installed-manifest.md:346). That violates CR-5/CR-8/CR-12 unless AGENTS.md is explicitly promoted into Section 0 with cite-class evidence or regenerated from an upstream standard source.

3. P1: Multiple installed rows are stale or still carry D6 `@latest` risk after install.  
Examples: codex manifest says `0.129.0` in [docs/sota-installed-manifest.md](Z:/claude-sota-installed/docs/sota-installed-manifest.md:59), but `codex --version` and provenance show `0.130.0` in [docs/install-provenance.md](Z:/claude-sota-installed/docs/install-provenance.md:10922). Claude row still says native `2.1.132`/latest `2.1.133` at manifest line 51, while the native eee binary is `2.1.138`. `ast-grep` is marked installed but still uses `@latest` in [docs/sota-installed-manifest.md](Z:/claude-sota-installed/docs/sota-installed-manifest.md:407), and npm currently reports `0.42.1` while installed is `0.42.0`. Phoenix MCP is unpinned `npx -y @arizeai/phoenix-mcp` in [.mcp.json](Z:/claude-sota-installed/.mcp.json:86).

4. P1: Probe-4 namespace collisions are real, not just theoretical.  
Local sibling-derived agents exist for `architect`, `code-reviewer`, `debugger`, and `verifier`, while enabled plugins also ship overlapping agents/skills. Example: local [.claude/agents/code-reviewer.md](Z:/claude-sota-installed/.claude/agents/code-reviewer.md:13) admits upstream parity, and enabled plugins are listed in [.claude/settings.json](Z:/claude-sota-installed/.claude/settings.json:315). A scan found 40 cached `code-reviewer.md` files. Marketplace aliases also drift: `anthropics/skills` is `anthropic-agent-skills` in known marketplaces but `skills` in settings; `anthropics/financial-services` is `claude-for-financial-services` vs `financial-services`.

5. P2: CR-1 cite-trail quality is uneven.  
Sample passes: CWC row resolves full SHA/files at [docs/sota-installed-manifest.md](Z:/claude-sota-installed/docs/sota-installed-manifest.md:75), GitHub MCP resolves file:line + SHA at line 142. Sample weak/fail rows: mcp-memory has URL-only upstream in line 92; vertical marketplace rows use short SHAs + homepage URLs at lines 565-567; several installed rows cite npm/GitHub homepage only at lines 573-590. CCBP and Context7 recorded SHAs resolve, but local dependency HEADs have advanced, so `@ HEAD <old-sha>` labels are stale.

**Phase 2 Blockers**
Tier 0: Section 10 still has many `PLANNED` CLI rows at [docs/sota-installed-manifest.md](Z:/claude-sota-installed/docs/sota-installed-manifest.md:155).  
Tier 1a: codex hooks are `INSTALLED-PARTIAL`, not full T1-T7 smoke PASS.  
Tier 1b/1c: `sota-researcher`, `safety_guard.py`, and `agent_plan_readonly_bash_guard.py` are `INSTALLED-AMBER`, not clean `INSTALLED`.  
Tier 2: Qdrant/Graphiti/FalkorDB are staged/partial, Exa/Perplexity/Firecrawl/arXiv remain `PLANNED`, and Section 7 has stale `PLANNED` rows despite live MCP entries.

**Prescribed Edits**
Reconcile CR-7 first: either downgrade `defaultMode` to the admissible phase or update manifest rows with real smoke PASS evidence before claiming Phase 3. Then fix stale installed rows, pin installed `@latest` commands, pin Phoenix MCP, normalize marketplace aliases, and resolve local/plugin agent collisions by removing, renaming, or explicitly documenting precedence for each duplicate.

Example: local [.claude/agents/code-reviewer.md](Z:/claude-sota-installed/.claude/agents/code-reviewer.md:13) admits upstream parity, and enabled plugins are listed in [.claude/settings.json](Z:/claude-sota-installed/.claude/settings.json:315). A scan found 40 cached `code-reviewer.md` files. Marketplace aliases also drift: `anthropics/skills` is `anthropic-agent-skills` in known marketplaces but `skills` in settings; `anthropics/financial-services` is `claude-for-financial-services` vs `financial-services`.

5. P2: CR-1 cite-trail quality is uneven.  
Sample passes: CWC row resolves full SHA/files at [docs/sota-installed-manifest.md](Z:/claude-sota-installed/docs/sota-installed-manifest.md:75), GitHub MCP resolves file:line + SHA at line 142. Sample weak/fail rows: mcp-memory has URL-only upstream in line 92; vertical marketplace rows use short SHAs + homepage URLs at lines 565-567; several installed rows cite npm/GitHub homepage only at lines 573-590. CCBP and Context7 recorded SHAs resolve, but local dependency HEADs have advanced, so `@ HEAD <old-sha>` labels are stale.

**Phase 2 Blockers**
Tier 0: Section 10 still has many `PLANNED` CLI rows at [docs/sota-installed-manifest.md](Z:/claude-sota-installed/docs/sota-installed-manifest.md:155).  
Tier 1a: codex hooks are `INSTALLED-PARTIAL`, not full T1-T7 smoke PASS.  
Tier 1b/1c: `sota-researcher`, `safety_guard.py`, and `agent_plan_readonly_bash_guard.py` are `INSTALLED-AMBER`, not clean `INSTALLED`.  
Tier 2: Qdrant/Graphiti/FalkorDB are staged/partial, Exa/Perplexity/Firecrawl/arXiv remain `PLANNED`, and Section 7 has stale `PLANNED` rows despite live MCP entries.

**Prescribed Edits**
Reconcile CR-7 first: either downgrade `defaultMode` to the admissible phase or update manifest rows with real smoke PASS evidence before claiming Phase 3. Then fix stale installed rows, pin installed `@latest` commands, pin Phoenix MCP, normalize marketplace aliases, and resolve local/plugin agent collisions by removing, renaming, or explicitly documenting precedence for each duplicate.
